# Ascendance 3.36.21 mechanics audit

**Evidence sources:** The complete 320-browser/238-Node audit and card observations below were produced at application checkpoint `9c89ea2441dde5b6689479e5faf9e815e411fcee` ([hosted full run](https://github.com/RubberDuckieGod/ascendance-game/actions/runs/41156645878)); its tested PR merge revision was `84abd049c46009db107472e23122899d4f178fbe`. They are not a full audit of the later application code.

Current application code `e71fc8773c575cf3667ef387e8b18cbd812def09` has **240 passing Node checks and 29 passing focused browser scenarios**, independently verified in [current focused CI](https://github.com/RubberDuckieGod/ascendance-game/actions/runs/41159829826). The current-source **322-case full run remains pending** at [41159829828](https://github.com/RubberDuckieGod/ascendance-game/actions/runs/41159829828). Current named outcomes and measurements are in [quest-transition-current-results.json](quest-transition-current-results.json), with reproduction and exact scope in [QUEST_TRANSITIONS_33626.md](QUEST_TRANSITIONS_33626.md). Vanguard's printed target-next-turn expiry remains **PARTIAL / UNVERIFIED**. The 657-record strict full-effect coverage gate remains incomplete; see [validation provenance](quest-transition-validation.json) for the separate non-passing system-browser attempt and its five-case follow-up.

---


This branch repairs the confirmed audit defects and implements the ten previously unsupported items. See IMPLEMENTED_RULES.md for reviewed/default rule definitions. Full effect certification remains incomplete. The downtime, Snare and reward follow-up is documented in DOWNTIME_SNARE_33622.md; the ordinary-action Gold rollback correction is in RESOURCE_ROLLBACK_33623.md. Regional deck, Blight Spores, and location-layout corrections are documented in ENCOUNTERS_LAYOUT_33624.md. Enemy-phase refresh and Search Action fixes are documented in PHASE_SEARCH_SYNC_33625.md. Atomic next-Quest setup, Lost Hero replacement, Encounter prompts and combat Reputation follow-up are documented in QUEST_TRANSITIONS_33626.md. Gameplay tests did not mutate production or staging player data. All functional requests target a local server with a new temporary persistence directory and isolated browser contexts. Deployment status is recorded separately from these isolated tests.

## Reproduction

Node 24; npm ci; npx playwright install --with-deps chromium; npm run audit:mechanics. On this cloud machine use CHROMIUM_PATH=/usr/bin/chromium. npm test runs the new checked-in foundation simulator. npm run stability:33621 runs the full browser suite. These are explicit replacements; the original historical suites were not recovered. The strict effect coverage gate remains separate from scenario pass/fail results.

## Remaining coverage gaps

Ability effects at levels 1–16, every branch/trigger/expiry of all 323 cards, all equipment and loot effects, full Enemy AI and targeting matrix, all campaign quests, concurrent reaction timing, and adversarial synchronization remain unverified except for explicit named assertions above. The generic dispatcher sweep must not be substituted for those tests. Staging was not used for mutations. Local execution is recorded here; hosted workflow and deployment outcomes are reported separately. A new-task environment restore remains unverified.
